(H-L)\ntask_years = [ws_task[f'{get_column_letter(c)}10'].value for c in range(8, 13)]\n\ndef percentile_inc(values, k):\n    # Excel PERCENTILE() == PERCENTILE.INC\n    xs = sorted(values)\n    n = len(xs)\n    if n == 0:\n        return None\n    if k <= 0:\n        return xs[0]\n    if k >= 1:\n        return xs[-1]\n    p = k * (n - 1)\n    i = int(p)\n    f = p - i\n    if i >= n - 1:\n        return xs[-1]\n    return xs[i] * (1 - f) + xs[i + 1] * f\n\n# Compute values for all target cells\ncomputed = {}\n\n# Lookup blocks\ndef compute_lookup_block(r1, r2):\n    for r in range(r1, r2 + 1):\n        code = ws_task[f'D{r}'].value\n        for idx, c in enumerate(range(8, 13)):\n            year = int(task_years[idx])\n            addr = f'{get_column_letter(c)}{r}'\n            computed[addr] = data_map[(str(code), year)]\n\ncompute_lookup_block(12, 17)\ncompute_lookup_block(19, 24)\ncompute_lookup_block(26, 31)\n\n# Headroom rows 35-40\nfor i, r in enumerate(range(35, 41)):\n    r_open = 12 + i\n    r_used = 19 + i\n    r_beds = 26 + i\n    for c in range(8, 13):\n        col = get_column_letter(c)\n        open_v = computed[f'{col}{r_open}']\n        used_v = labels\n# Build per-year headroom arrays\nheadroom_by_col = {}\nfor c in range(8, 13):\n    col = get_column_letter(c)\n    headroom_by_col[col] = [computed[f'{col}{r}'] for r in range(35, 41)]\n\nfor 2.0  # n=6\n        elif lbl == 'simple mean':\n            computed[addr] = sum(xs) / len(xs)\n        elif lbl == '25th percentile':\n            computed[addr] = percentile_inc(xs, 0.25)\n        elif lbl == '75th percentile':\n            computed[addr] = percentile_inc(xs, 0.75)\n\n# Weighted mean row 50\nfor c in range(8, 13):\n    col = get_column_letter(c)\n    vals = [computed[f'{col}{r}'] for r in range(35, 41)]\n    wts = [computed[f'{col}{r}'] for r in range(26, 32)]\n    num = sum(v * w for v, w in zip(vals, wts))\n    den = sum(wts)\n    computed[f'{col}50'] = num / den\n\n# Collect all cells we wrote formulas into\nformula_cells = set()\nfor r in list(range(12, 18)) + list(range(19, 25)) + list(range(26, 32)) + list(range(35, 41)) + list(range(42, 48)) + xs[-1]\n    p = k * (n - 1)\n    i = int(p)\n    f = p - i\n    if i >= n - 1:\n        return xs[-1]\n    return xs[i] * (1 - f) + xs[i + 1] * f\n\ncomputed = {}\n\ndef compute_lookup_block(r1, elif lbl == 'median':\n            s = sorted(xs)\n            computed[addr] = (s[2] + s[3]) / 2.0\n        elif lbl == 'simple mean':\n            computed[addr] = sum(xs) / len(xs)\n        elif
